*Mikołaj Słowikowski,Maciej Witold Majewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究解码器-only语言模型的隐藏状态反演问题，提出了一种连续嵌入空间优化方法，通过软代理逐步逼近目标隐藏状态而不进行硬投影，仅在内部循环结束时才提交离散token。该方法暴露了丰富的内部信号（排名轨迹、损失曲线、离散损失），并发现重构失败存在尖锐的类别不对称性：空间前缀化的高频功能词占主导，而内容词几乎完美恢复。在10-token C4提示上，精确匹配率从66.9%提升至97.5%，表明大多数错误是可恢复的近失而非真正的歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究从解码器-only语言模型的最后一层隐藏状态恢复原始输入token序列的问题，旨在探索连续空间优化方法相较于传统硬投影方法的优势，并分析重构失败的内在原因。

Method: 采用连续嵌入空间优化方法，使用软代理在连续空间中逐步逼近目标隐藏状态，不进行任何硬token投影，仅在内部循环结束时一次性提交token。通过分析排名轨迹、逐位置损失曲线和提交时的离散损失来评估重构质量。

Result: 在10-token C4提示上，精确匹配率从66.9%提升至97.5%，平均相似度0.994；发现空间前缀化的高频功能词密集嵌入区域主导失败，内容词几乎完美恢复；与SIPIT参考方法的对比表明连续公式化使优化可观测且失败可检测。

Conclusion: 最后一层隐藏状态对GPT-2而言具有与原始文本同等的敏感性；连续空间优化方法虽比逐步骤硬投影慢，但提供了可观测性和失败检测能力；大多数重构错误是可恢复的近失而非真正的语义歧义。

Abstract: This work studies the hidden-state inversion problem: recovering the original input token sequence of a decoder-only language model from its last-layer hidden states. Rather than treating inversion as a one-shot reconstruction, we study it as a continuous embedding-space optimisation in which a soft proxy is driven towards the leaked target without any hard-token projection during the search, and a token is committed only once, at the end of the inner loop. This design choice has two consequences which are the main focus of this paper. First, keeping the optimisation entirely in continuous space exposes a rich set of internal signals: rank trajectories of the ground-truth token, per-position loss curves, and a discrete loss measured at commit time. Second, the discrete loss allows assessing the correctness of recovery via cumulative discrete loss. We further analyse which tokens break the reconstructions and find a sharp categorical asymmetry: space-prefixed, high-frequency function words in dense regions of the embedding matrix dominate the failures, while content-bearing tokens are recovered almost perfectly. On 10-token C4 prompts the exact-match rate rises from 66.9% to 97.5% (mean similarity 0.994) as the candidate window is widened, confirming that most errors are recoverable near-misses rather than genuine ambiguities. A comparison with the released SIPIT reference situates these findings: per-step hard projection is faster, but the continuous formulation is what makes the optimisation observable and its failures detectable. The results show that last-layer hidden states of GPT-2 are as sensitive as the original text.

</details>


### [39] [CAT: Confidence-Adaptive Thinking for Efficient Reasoning of Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00862)
*Qizhi Jiang,Shuo Wang,Pei Ke,Yuhang Song,Ke Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Confidence-Adaptive Thinking (CAT) 框架，通过将模型内在的自信心信号融入偏好优化，使大推理模型能根据问题难度自主调节推理长度，在压缩简单问题的推理链的同时保持困难问题的准确性，从而在多个基准上实现推理准确率与效率的更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型在处理简单问题时常出现"过度思考"（overthinking），导致大量 token 开销和推理效率下降。现有压缩方法要么采用统一长度缩减，要么依赖粗粒度的难度估计，往往在困难问题上性能下降严重。

Method: 提出 Confidence-Adaptive Thinking (CAT) 框架，将模型内在的自信心信号作为置信度融入偏好优化过程，使模型能够根据问题难度自主调节推理链长度。

Result: 在多个基准和不同基座模型上，CAT 在推理准确性方面持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: CAT 使 LRMs 能够有效压缩高置信度回答的推理链，同时保留对不确定问题的充分推理，为工业场景中平衡精度与延迟提供了一种鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have achieved remarkable success on complex tasks by leveraging long chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories, yet they frequently exhibit overthinking on simple queries, resulting in significant token overhead and reduced inference efficiency. However, existing compression methods predominantly apply uniform length reduction or rely on coarse-grained difficulty estimation, often leading to performance degradation on difficult problems. To address this limitation, we propose Confidence-Adaptive Thinking (CAT), a framework that incorporates the model's intrinsic self-certainty signals as confidence into the preference optimization process, which autonomously modulates reasoning lengths based on problem difficulty. Experimental results show that CAT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on reasoning accuracy across multiple benchmarks on different base models. Our work enables LRMs to effectively compress confident responses while deliberating on uncertain ones, offering a potentially robust solution for balancing accuracy and latency in practical industrial scenarios.

</details>


### [40] [Dynamic Bidirectional Pattern Memory: A Production-Scale Empirical Characterisation of Inference-Time Gating in Clinical NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00870)
*Ali H. Lazem,William Teahan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了生产级临床NLP流水线中推理阶段的模式-记忆门控机制，发现最自然的记忆设计（从验证器拒绝中学习过滤规则）在大规模场景下会静默失败，而成功的关键在于过滤器必须测试与验证器相同的证据，而非模仿其输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成器-验证器双模型流水线中，验证器每次都需要重新评估所有候选提取结果，计算开销大。研究者希望引入一个轻量级记忆模块，在学习部署过程中学会过滤那些已知会失败的提取结果，从而避免验证器重复检查。

Method: 在167,034篇PMC-Patients叙事文本上部署Llama-3.3 70B作为生成器、MMed-Llama-3.1 70B作为验证器，并添加一个轻量级记忆模块。测试了五种不同的问答过滤器变体及一种基于临床本体的关系提取过滤器。

Result: 1) 从验证器拒绝中直接学习过滤规则在大规模场景下失败（785,797条拒绝记录未能积累有效规则）；2) 基于固定临床本体的规则过滤器无需验证器即可捕捉49,734条违反本体的关系；3) 五种QA过滤器中仅一种成功，即检查患者实体是否支持所问问题，成功过滤器的标记准确率是验证器拒绝率的1.84倍；4) 所有过滤器有效的共同条件：必须测试与验证器相同的证据。

Conclusion: 生成器-验证器流水线中，最自然的记忆设计（从验证器输出学习）可能在大规模场景下静默失败；过滤器的选择性在工程实施之前就已由信号是否探测验证器真正回答的问题所决定；系统采用标记而非删除策略，保证了临床可审查性。

Abstract: We study inference-time pattern-memory gating in a production-scale clinical natural language processing (NLP) pipeline. The pipeline pairs a generator (Llama-3.3 70B) proposing extractions with a verifier (MMed-Llama-3.1 70B) accepting or rejecting them, over 167,034 PMC-Patients narratives, and adds a lightweight memory that learns at deployment which extractions to filter, so the verifier need not re-examine candidates already seen to fail. We report four findings. First, learning filtering rules directly from the verifier's rejections failed at full scale: the relation-extraction filter stayed empty despite 785,797 logged rejections, because they were spread too thinly across too many distinct forms to accumulate. Second, a simpler rule using a fixed clinical ontology produced the same filtering without the verifier, capturing 49,734 ontology-violating relations on a held-out 5,000-patient set. Third, of five versions of the question-answering filter, four failed for distinct, instructive reasons; the fifth succeeded by checking whether a patient's extracted entities support the question asked, and where it applies was 1.84 times likelier to flag an answer the verifier would reject than one it would accept. Fourth, one pattern held across all five: a filter is selective only when it tests the same evidence the verifier weighs, not when it imitates the verifier's output. Together these give a transferable result for any generator-verifier pipeline: the most natural memory design can fail silently at scale, and whether a pre-generation gate is selective is decided before any engineering effort, by whether its signal probes the question the verifier itself answers. Throughout, the system flags suspect extractions rather than deleting them, so every decision stays visible for clinical review. All code and test artefacts are released openly.

</details>


### [41] [How Ethos and Pathos Appeals Resonate in Reader Interpretations of Social Media Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00873)
*Ewelina Gajewska,Katarzyna Budzynska,Jaroslaw Chudziak,Liesbeth Allein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析社交媒体句子及其人工撰写的解释，发现30%的情况下观众对修辞策略（ethos和pathos）的解释与原文存在分歧，且修辞内容比中性内容引发更大的解释变异性；同时，原文中的修辞手法能预测观众对作者的态度，揭示了修辞如何在沉默的多数读者中塑造感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注社交媒体帖子和评论中的显性互动，忽略了占读者大多数的"普遍受众"（沉默读者）如何理解和接受修辞信息。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究古典修辞策略（ethos和pathos）如何在沉默受众的意义解释中产生共鸣。

Method: 使用社交媒体句子与人工撰写的解释配对数据集，对原文和解释均进行ethos和pathos的标注，评估修辞诉求在解释过程中是否得到保留。

Result: 30%的情况下解释与原文存在分歧；修辞性内容比中性内容引发更大的解释变异性；原文中的ethos和pathos能够预测观众对作者的态度。

Conclusion: 修辞策略以微妙的方式塑造了沉默受众的感知，这种影响超越了可见的互动（如评论、点赞等），揭示了研究普遍受众反应的重要性。

Abstract: Rhetorical strategies and their influence on audiences are often studied through social media posts and comments. However, this focus overlooks the universal audience, which is the majority of readers who remain silent and do not explicitly express how a message affects them. This study investigates how two classical modes of persuasion, ethos and pathos, resonate in the silent audience's interpretations of meaning. Using a dataset of social media sentences paired with human-written interpretations, we label both sources for ethos and pathos and assess whether these rhetorical appeals are preserved. Our analyses show that interpretations diverge from the original sentences in 30% of cases, with rhetorically charged content eliciting greater variability than neutral content. We further find that ethos and pathos in original sentences can predict audience attitudes toward the author, underscoring the subtle ways rhetoric shapes perception beyond visible engagement.

</details>


### [42] [MultiSynt/MT: Trillion-Token Multi-Parallel Pre-Training Data Translated Across 36 Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00890)
*Maximilian Idahl,Jörg Tiedemann,Sampo Pyysalo,David Salinas,Tomasz Galica,Shenbin Qian,Tudor Nicolae Mateiu,Zihao Li,Anna Lokrantz,Fedor Vitiugin,André F. T. Martins,Jenna Kanerva,Filip Ginter,Matthias Lindemann,Tim Isbister,Birger Moell,Jonas Lindh,Jan Hajič,Jenia Jitsev,Andrey Kutuzov,Stephan Oepen,Gema Ramírez-Sánchez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MultiSynt/MT是一个覆盖36种欧洲语言的约4.8万亿目标语言token的开放合成平行语料库，通过翻译1000亿高质量Nemotron-CC tokens构建。在该语料库上训练的LLM能以少约72%的预训练token达到HPLT 2.0原生数据基线的最终分数，在匹配的100B token训练预算下相对提升约15%。研究还发现了标准多选题评估的盲点，并发布了包含多系统行对齐翻译的语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开放的大规模预训练语料库主要集中在英语，限制了多语言大语言模型的发展，因此需要构建覆盖更多语言的高质量开放预训练资源。

Method: 使用Tower+和OPUS-MT/HPLT-MT翻译系统，将1000亿高质量Nemotron-CC tokens翻译为36种欧洲语言，构建约4.8万亿目标语言token的合成平行语料库MultiSynt/MT。

Result: 参考LLM在MultiSynt/MT上训练后，能以少约72%的预训练token达到HPLT 2.0原生数据基线的最终分数；在匹配100B token训练预算下相对提升约15%。标准多选题评估存在盲点，遗漏翻译质量差异，而流利度敏感的LLM-as-judge评估能有效捕捉这些差异；挪威语中习语和文化相关任务仍更适合原生数据。

Conclusion: MultiSynt/MT为多种中低资源欧洲语言提供了目前最大的开放预训练资源，能显著提升训练效率，但标准评估存在盲点，需要更敏感的评估方法，某些语言特定任务仍需原生数据。语料库已开放发布以支持可控研究。

Abstract: Open web-scale pre-training corpora remain concentrated in English, limiting multilingual LLM development. We introduce MultiSynt/MT, an open synthetic parallel corpus with approximately 4.8 trillion target-language tokens across 36 European languages, produced by translating 100 billion high-quality Nemotron-CC tokens with Tower+ and OPUS-MT/HPLT-MT systems. For many medium- and lower-resource European languages, this is the largest openly available pre-training resource. On a broad multilingual benchmark suite, reference LLMs trained on MultiSynt/MT reach the final score of HPLT 2.0, a native-data baseline, using roughly 72% fewer pre-training tokens, and outperform it by approximately 15% relative at a matched 100B-token training budget. Our analyses also identify evaluation blind spots: standard multiple-choice benchmarks miss translation-quality differences that a fluency-sensitive LLM-as-judge evaluation cleanly recovers on the trained LLMs (with no fluency deficit in MultiSynt itself), and Norwegian idiomatic and culturally grounded tasks remain better served by native data. We release the corpus, including row-aligned translations from multiple systems, to support controlled research on multilingual pre-training data and evaluation.

</details>


### [43] [Beyond Document Grounding: Span-Level Hallucination Detection over Code, Tool Output, and Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00895)
*Ádám Kovács,Bowei He,Xue Liu,István Boros,Szilveszter Tóth,Gábor Recski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个覆盖代码、工具输出、结构化文档及自然语言的统一幻觉检测基准，并基于Qwen3.5-2B微调的检测器在统一测试集上达到0.689 span-F1，在代码Agent场景下显著优于现有方法（0.60 vs LettuceDetect-large的0.17），同时在自然语言基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG幻觉检测研究主要针对自然语言文档证据，但实际应用中的生成系统越来越多依赖结构化输入（源代码、开发工具输出、Markdown文档、表格、仓库元数据等），缺乏统一的评估基准和有效的检测方法。

Method: 构建统一基准：从已有正确回答出发，注入带精确字符级别标注的局部幻觉，并对代码测试子集进行基于证据的人工审核验证。基于Qwen3.5-2B微调幻觉检测模型。

Result: 微调后的Qwen3.5-2B检测器在统一测试集上达到0.689 span-F1，在代码Agent来源上达到0.60，大幅超越LettuceDetect-large（0.17）和最强零样本LLM评判（≤0.22）；在自然语言基准上RAGTruth example-F1为81.8，English PsiloQA IoU为0.724。

Conclusion: 所提方法在结构化输入幻觉检测上取得显著提升，同时保持自然语言任务上的竞争性能，验证了统一基准和微调策略的有效性。

Abstract: Hallucination detection for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is usually evaluated on natural-language document evidence. However, grounded generation systems increasingly rely on structured inputs: source code, developer-tool output, markdown documents, tables, and repository metadata. We introduce a unified benchmark for span-level hallucination detection over code, tool output, structured documents, and existing natural-language RAG datasets. The benchmark is built by starting from grounded correct answers, injecting localized hallucinations with exact character labels, and validating the code test split with evidence-based review. Our fine-tuned Qwen3.5-2B detector reaches 0.689 span-F1 on the unified test set and 0.60 on the code-agent source, where it substantially outperforms LettuceDetect-large (0.17) and the strongest zero-shot LLM judges we evaluated (at most 0.22). The same model remains competitive on established natural-language benchmarks, with 81.8 RAGTruth example-F1 and 0.724 English PsiloQA IoU.

</details>


### [44] [From Personas to Plot: Character-Grounded Multi-Agent Story Generation for Long-Form Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00918)
*Aayush Aluru,Chloe Ho,Muhammad Hammouri,Kerry Luo,Myra Malik,Ryan Lagasse,Arjun Bahuguna,Vasu Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MAGNET（多智能体目标驱动叙事引擎）和ATLAS（基于图的幻觉检测管线）的统一框架，通过显式世界状态追踪与多智能体协作生成长篇故事，在100页长度下相比基线模型减少41%注释和50%幻觉，相比IBSEN减少34%注释和45%幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在长篇故事生成中难以维持叙事一致性和连贯的情节线，现有方法缺乏对故事世界状态的显式追踪和多智能体协作机制。

Method: MAGNET采用基于角色的智能体在共享世界状态下提出行动，结合演进的故事目标驱动生成；ATLAS通过图结构比较场景级世界表征来检测幻觉。

Result: 100页故事中MAGNET比单模型基线减少41%注释和50%幻觉，比IBSEN减少34%注释和45%幻觉，成对评估结果相似。

Conclusion: 长篇叙事可以从显式的世界状态追踪和目标驱动的多智能体生成中涌现，为可控且结构连贯的长篇故事生成奠定基础。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive creative fiction generation, they struggle to maintain narrative consistency and coherent plot lines in long-form stories. In this work, we introduce a unified framework for long-form narrative generation and verification. MAGNET, a multi-agent goal-driven narrative engine for storytelling, generates stories with persona-grounded character agents that propose actions based on a shared world state and evolving story goals, while ATLAS is a graph-based pipeline that compares scene-level world representations across a generated story to detect hallucinations. By evaluating MAGNET using an LLM editor, pairwise rubric scoring, and ATLAS, we show that our framework produces coherent narratives compared to single-model prompting and IBSEN. At 100 pages, MAGNET reduced annotations and hallucinations by 41 and 50%, respectively, compared to the single model baseline and by 34 and 45%, respectively, compared to IBSEN, with pairwise rubric evaluation showing similar results. These results suggest that long-form narratives can emerge from explicit world-state tracking and goal-driven multi-agent generation, providing a foundation for controllable and structurally coherent long-form narrative generation.

</details>


### [45] [Persona Non Grata: LLM Persona-Driven Generations in MCQA are Unstable in Distinct Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00937)
*César Guerra-Solano,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在多项选择题回答（MCQA）任务中角色驱动生成（PDG）的不稳定性问题，提出了三个评估指标（性能稳定性、结果稳定性和问题正确性稳定性），发现不稳定性在不同模型家族、规模、问题领域间存在显著差异，且任务提示格式比温度等超参数引发更多预测不稳定性，不稳定性与任务准确性相关，不同实验设置会导致不同的最佳/最差角色表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注自由文本对话中角色表达的一致性，但非文本密集型输出（如MCQA）中的角色稳定性问题被忽视，本文旨在填补这一空白，探究LLM在MCQA任务中角色驱动生成的不稳定性。

Method: 开发了三个度量指标：性能稳定性、结果稳定性和问题正确性稳定性，从三个不同维度评估PDG的不稳定性，并在不同模型家族、模型规模、问题领域以及超参数设置下进行实验分析。

Result: 不稳定性在不同模型家族和模型规模间存在一致性差异；数学/常识问题导致更大的不稳定性；任务提示格式比温度等其他超参数引入更多预测不稳定性；不稳定性与任务准确性相关；不同实验设置下同一任务的最佳/最差角色表现不同。

Conclusion: 揭示了在角色驱动生成中检查超参数不稳定性的重要性，表明即使相似设置下角色表现也可能存在显著差异，需要关注MCQA任务中的稳定性问题。

Abstract: Persona-driven generations (PDGs) have seen prolific use in research and industry applications, where a large language model (LLM) takes on a 'persona' while completing some task. While persona expressed through free-form text (like dialogue) has substantial work investigating stability or consistency, relatively, persona expressed in non-text-heavy outputs (like in multiple-choice question answering, or MCQA) is often overlooked. We work to address this gap, seeking to understand the instability of LLM PDGs in MCQA tasks. We develop three metrics investigating the performance, outcome, and question correctness stability, evaluating three distinct dimensions. Using these metrics, we find that instability varies consistently between model families and model size, and across question domains, with math/commonsense questions leading to greater instability. We also find task prompt format introduces more prediction instability than other hyperparameters, like temperature. Finally, we find that instability is related to task accuracy, and using our instability metrics, find different experimental settings that result in different best and worst personas for tasks, despite their similarity. This reveals the importance of checking hyperparameter instability in PDGs.

</details>


### [46] [Quantifying the Affective Gap: A Zero-Shot Evaluation of LLMs on Fine-Grained Emotion Taxonomies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00968)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Jessica M. Johnson,Vikas Ashok,Sachin Shetty,Sampath Jayarathna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对Claude、ChatGPT、Gemini三大商用大模型进行零样本细粒度13类情感分类评估，发现Gemini准确率最高（39.9%），所有模型在sarcasm和desire上表现优异，在love、confusion、shame上普遍失败，且模型间无显著统计差异，表明当前前沿AI系统在零样本细粒度情感分类上存在共同性能天花板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感识别是人机交互、心理健康支持和对话AI中的基础挑战，现有研究缺乏对最新商用大模型在零样本细粒度情感分类任务上的系统评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从boltuix/emotions数据集的131,306条句子中分层抽样1,000条（覆盖13类情感），通过统一无示例提示词，分别调用Claude、ChatGPT、Gemini的生产API进行零样本分类，使用准确率、宏F1分数和McNemar检验进行统计比较。

Result: Gemini准确率39.9%、宏F1=0.363；GPT-5.4准确率38.8%、宏F1=0.291；Claude准确率38.0%、宏F1=0.159。所有模型在sarcasm和desire上表现最佳，在love、confusion、shame上最差。McNemar检验无显著差异（p>0.10），Claude的宏F1显著偏低暴露出类别不平衡预测偏差。

Conclusion: 当前前沿商用大模型在零样本细粒度情感分类上存在共同性能上限（约40%准确率），模型间无显著差异，且普遍存在对特定情感类别的识别困难，需进一步研究优化策略。

Abstract: Emotion recognition in natural language is a foundational challenge in affective computing, with critical implications for human-computer interaction, mental health support, and conversational AI. This paper presents a rigorous, unified zero-shot evaluation of three leading commercial large language models: Claude (claude-sonnet-4-6), ChatGPT (GPT-5.4), and Gemini (gemini-2.5-flash). The models were queried through their respective production APIs as of April 2026 on a fine-grained 13-class emotion classification task. Using a stratified 1,000-sentence sample from the boltuix/emotions dataset, which comprises 131,306 sentences across 13 categories, a single uniform prompt with no exemplars was applied identically across all models. Gemini achieves the highest accuracy (39.9%) and macro-F1 score (0.363), followed by GPT-5.4 (38.8%, macro-F1 = 0.291) and Claude (38.0%, macro-F1 = 0.159). All models excel on sarcasm and desire while consistently failing on love, confusion, and shame. McNemar tests reveal no statistically significant pairwise differences (p > 0.10), suggesting convergence at a shared zero-shot ceiling. Claude's markedly lower macro-F1 score exposes a class-imbalance prediction bias. These findings highlight the current limitations of frontier AI systems in zero-shot fine-grained emotion classification.

</details>


### [47] [Svarna: An Open Corpus Workbench for Modern Greek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00970)
*Stergios Chatzikyriakidis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Svarna 是一个面向现代希腊语的开源网络语料库工作台，整合了5个数据库、超5.07亿词和约2900万句子，提供词频、搭配、话语标记、注册比较等多种分析功能，基于SQLite FTS5和FastAPI构建，采用MIT许可证发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希腊语语言技术长期存在资源分散、访问受限或不可用等空白问题，现有语料库散落于不同平台且多数需要机构权限或已下线，亟需一个统一、开放、无需登录和安装的集成平台。

Method: 整合5个覆盖不同注册（机构、文学、方言、社交媒体、历史）的语料库；提供KWIC索引、频率分析（含注册归一化）、基于互信息的搭配提取、93个话语标记词典及分布画像、n-gram/变体/搭配网络等文本级分析工具、基于log-ratio的注册比较、正则搜索及可选的LLM层；后端采用SQLite FTS5全文索引+FastAPI，打包为Docker容器部署于Azure。

Result: 构建了一个包含5.07亿词、2900万句子的集成语料库，支持多种语言学分析功能，用户无需登录即可使用，且支持自定义语料库和独立部署。

Conclusion: Svarna 是探索现代希腊语语料数据的第一步，为未来更全面的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces Svarna, a free, open-source, web-based corpus workbench for modern Greek. Svarna integrates five databases covering various registers, institutional, literary, dialectal, social media, and historical, to provide a total of more than 507 million words and around 29 million sentences. This platform addresses the chronic gaps in Greek language technology. Although various corpus resources exist, they are scattered across different platforms, and in many cases, institutional access is restricted or they are no longer available online. Svarna integrates these resources into a single interface that can be used without logging in, installation, or specialized training. This system provides a concordancer with KWIC marking capabilities, frequency analysis including register-by-register normalization, collocation extraction using mutual information, a dictionary of 93 Greek discourse markers providing distribution profiles, text-level analysis tools including n-grams, variants, and collocation networks, register comparison using log-ratio, regular expression search, and an optional LLM layer for pragmatic annotation and free research mode. This platform is built upon SQLite FTS5 full-text indexes provided via a FastAPI backend, deployed as Docker containers on Azure, and released under the MIT license. Source code, build scripts, and deployment configurations are publicly available on GitHub. Users can add their own corpora and deploy their own instances. This document describes the system design, corpus structure, and use cases demonstrating the various queries supported by the platform. Svarna serves as the first step in exploring available data and is expected to lay the foundation for more comprehensive research in the future.

</details>


### [48] [KnowledgeDebugger -- an Exploration Tool for Knowledge Localization and Editing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01000)
*Eric Benz,Lennart Stöpler,Nikolai Bolik,Artur Andrzejak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了KnowledgeDebugger，一个基于GUI的探索工具，用于Transformer中的知识定位与编辑，支持无需代码即可使用EasyEdit库中的方法，并通过案例研究展示了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识编辑研究通常先基于单个样本进行现象探索，再开展大规模统计实验。第一阶段缺乏便捷的可视化探索工具，研究者需要编写代码才能使用现有的知识编辑方法库（如EasyEdit），降低了研究效率。

Method: 受LM-Debugger启发，构建KnowledgeDebugger图形界面工具，实现对EasyEdit库中多种最新知识编辑方法的无代码访问，支持知识定位和编辑的可视化探索。

Result: 通过案例研究展示了工具在复现和分析近期知识编辑领域发现方面的有效性。

Conclusion: KnowledgeDebugger填补了知识编辑研究中第一阶段探索性分析的工具空白，为研究人员提供了低门槛的可视化分析平台。

Abstract: Recent research has increasingly focused on understanding how Transformers store and process knowledge, as well as how this knowledge can be edited. Research work in this area is often conducted in two phases: first, phenomena are explored on individual samples. Then, when results appear promising, more statistically robust experiments follow. To support the first phase, we propose KnowledgeDebugger, a GUI-based exploration tool for knowledge localization and editing in Transformers. Our tool - inspired by LM-Debugger - offers no-code access to the methods in EasyEdit, a widely used library of state-of-the-art Knowledge Editing approaches. We demonstrate the tool's effectiveness through case studies of recent findings in this field.

</details>


### [49] [Logit-Contribution Scoring Identifies Non-Literal Retrieval Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01002)
*Aryo Pradipta Gema,Beatrice Alex,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Logit-Contribution Scoring (LOCOS) 方法，用于检测大语言模型中通过非字面复制方式（而非逐词复制）进行长上下文信息合成的注意力头。该方法通过评估注意力头输出值（OV）回路对答案 token 的 logit 贡献来检测，在 NoLiMa 等非字面检索基准上显著优于现有方法，且具有检索特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力头检测方法仅基于"注意力权重与生成 token 的匹配程度"（字面复制准则），无法检测那些通过 OV 回路实现非字面信息检索与合成的注意力头。理解这类头部对于解释长上下文模型行为至关重要。

Method: 提出 LOCOS 评分：在每个注意力头的 OV 回路输出上，计算其与答案 token 反嵌入向量方向的投影，对比 needle（相关信息）和 off-needle（无关信息）源位置，单次前向传播即可完成检测。

Result: 在 Qwen3、Gemma-3、OLMo-3.1 三个模型家族上，均值消融 top LOCOS 头部在 NoLiMa 基准上以更少的头部数量使 ROUGE-L 指标崩溃。Qwen3-8B 上消融 50 个头部使 ROUGE-L 从 0.401 降至 0.000，最强基线仍保留 0.292。消融效果具有检索特异性（参数回忆和算术推理不受影响），且在其他长上下文任务（MuSiQue、BABI-Long）上表现一致。

Conclusion: LOCOS 是一种有效的写感知（write-aware）注意力头检测方法，能够识别在长上下文中执行非字面信息合成的注意力头，填补了现有检测方法的空白。

Abstract: In long-context use, large language models frequently synthesize answers from the meaning of a relevant context span rather than literally copy-pasting them. Identifying which attention heads perform this synthesis matters for interpreting long-context model behavior. Yet existing detectors miss these heads by construction: they reward heads whose attended token matches the generated token, a literal-copy criterion that captures where a head reads but not what it writes through its output-value (OV) circuit, the very mechanism that carries non-literal retrieval. We introduce Logit-Contribution Scoring (LOCOS), a write-aware detector that scores each head by the projection of its OV-circuit output onto the answer-token unembedding direction, contrasting needle and off-needle source positions in a single forward pass. Across three model families (Qwen3, Gemma-3, OLMo-3.1), mean-ablating the top LOCOS heads on the NoLiMa non-literal retrieval benchmark collapses ROUGE-L at lower head counts than prior attention-based detections; on Qwen3-8B, ablating 50 heads drives ROUGE-L from 0.401 to 0.000 while the strongest baseline still retains 0.292. The selected heads are retrieval-specific: parametric recall and arithmetic reasoning stay at baseline under the same ablation. On Qwen3-8B, the same ablation also drops MuSiQue from 0.55 to 0.08 and BABI-Long from 0.62 to 0.20, while a random-heads control stays within 0.05 of baseline.

</details>


### [50] [Understanding Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01006)
*Yannik Keller,Thomas Eisenmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了大型语言模型（LLM）的最新进展，涵盖Transformer架构、涌现的类人认知能力（如符号推理、心智理论和欺骗策略）、可解释AI方法（神经元激活分析到电路追踪），以及关于LLM是否真正理解还是仅表面理解的争议。作者主张对LLM认知进行更细致的讨论，反对过度简化的人机差异否定或AI拟人化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在AI和自然语言处理领域取得重大突破，但其机制、能力及与人类认知的关系仍存在诸多争议和未解问题，亟需系统梳理当前理解并厘清辩论焦点。

Method: 本文为综述性研究，通过回顾近期关于LLM涌现能力的证据、Transformer架构机制、可解释AI方法（如神经元激活分析和电路追踪），以及对比正反两方面研究（成功案例与失败案例），系统梳理现有认识。

Result: LLM展现出符号推理、心智理论、欺骗策略等看似类人的认知能力，能解决传统上认为需要人类认知的任务；同时存在揭示人机差异的失败案例。可解释AI方法正从神经元层面到电路层面逐步揭示其内部机制。关于LLM是否真正理解还是仅模式记忆，存在对立观点。

Conclusion: 反对AI拟人化的观点（认为LLM行为仅是训练数据的模式记忆）受到对优化过程和认知能力误解的引导。作者主张更细致地讨论LLM认知，既不忽视人机差异，也不通过过度简化的还原论观点排除AI认知的可能性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) represent one of the most significant advances in AI and natural language processing in recent years. Still, many pressing questions about their mechanisms, capabilities, and relationship to human cognition remain highly debated. This chapter aims to outline our current understanding of LLMs by discussing recent evidence on emerging capabilities and their mechanistic implementation within processing layers. We begin with a concise overview of the Transformer architecture, emphasizing how the attention mechanism enables training on massive datasets, allowing LLMs to function as generalist rather than specialized models. Next, we examine emergent LLM capabilities that appear to resemble aspects of human cognition, including symbolic reasoning, theory of mind, and deception strategies. Several studies provide evidence that LLMs can solve tasks previously thought to require human-like cognition. Other studies reveal insightful failure cases that shed light on the differences between human and LLM cognition. Alongside these findings, we review explainable AI approaches ranging from neuron activation analysis to circuit tracing. In the final section, we address current debates concerning what LLMs genuinely understand versus what they merely appear to understand. Prominent arguments against AI anthropomorphism point to the simplicity of LLM training objectives, claiming that LLM behavior is better explained by pattern memorization of training data than by genuine cognition. We argue that this standpoint is guided by misconceptions about optimization processes and cognitive capacity, and advocate for a more nuanced discussion of LLM cognition that neither dismisses the differences between humans and LLMs nor precludes the possibility of AI cognition through overly simplistic reductionist arguments.

</details>


### [51] [Reading Order Inference for Complex Document Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01018)
*Iddo Hakim,Sharva Gogawale,Omer Ventura,Gal Grudka,Daria Vasyutinsky-Shapira,Berat Kurar-Barakat,Nachum Dershowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的图框架，用于推断复杂历史手稿（如Glossa Ordinaria布局）的阅读顺序。该方法将OCR文本行构建为有向候选转移图，通过因果语言模型条件似然和BERT下一句预测（NSP）两个轻量级语言模型信号的加权组合对边进行评分，并采用最大遗憾推理规则解决贪婪边缘选择中的“边缘窃取”问题。在合成Glossa布局、ALTO页面几何和多列英文数据集上，该方法显著优于递归XY-cut和LayoutReader基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史手稿数字化中，阅读顺序推断是一个关键瓶颈，尤其是在包含多个空间交错阅读流的复杂页面（如Glossa Ordinaria布局）中，现有方法难以处理非矩形、非凸区域内的文本环绕情况。

Method: 提出一种无训练的图框架：将OCR文本行作为有向候选转移图的节点，边由因果语言模型条件似然和BERT下一句预测（NSP）的加权组合评分；采用度约束有向路径覆盖恢复全局阅读顺序，并提出最大遗憾推理规则避免贪婪选择的级联失败。

Result: 在合成Glossa布局上恢复95%的真实后继边（XY-cut仅50%）；在OmniDocBench多列子集上达88%宏边准确率（XY-cut 75%，LayoutReader 25%）；在水平和垂直镜像反射下变化小于1个百分点，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的图框架有效解决了历史手稿中复杂阅读顺序推断问题，在多种布局上均显著优于现有方法，且具有镜像不变性。句子嵌入信号未带来提升，LayoutReader基线因粒度不匹配迁移效果差。

Abstract: Reading order inference remains a critical bottleneck in the digitization of complex historical manuscripts, where pages contain multiple spatially interleaved reading streams, the canonical example being the Glossa Ordinaria layout, in which a central text is surrounded by commentaries that wrap around it in non-rectangular, non-convex regions. We present a training-free, graph-based framework: each OCR text line becomes a node in a directed candidate-transition graph, edges are scored by a weighted additive ensemble of two lightweight language-model signals (causal language model conditional likelihood and BERT next-sentence prediction, NSP; a third sentence-embedding signal was evaluated but did not improve reading order), and the global reading order is recovered as a degree-constrained directed path cover. To avoid the cascading "edge-theft" failures of greedy edge selection, we propose a max-regret inference rule that prioritizes commitments with high opportunity cost. We evaluate on synthetic Glossa Ordinaria grid layouts, on 23 ALTO page geometries (10 historical source pages plus mirrored and flipped variants), and on a 140-page multi-column English subset of OmniDocBench, comparing our method against the canonical recursive XY-cut (PaddleOCR PP-StructureV3) and two LayoutReader variants (layout-only and text+layout) on identical inputs. On wrap-around Glossa layouts our method recovers 95% of ground-truth successor edges on average vs. XY-cut's 50%; on the OmniDocBench multi-column subset it reaches 88% macro edge accuracy versus XY-cut's 75% and LayoutReader's 25%. The LayoutReader baselines transfer poorly due to a word-level vs. line-level granularity mismatch. We additionally verify mirror-invariance under horizontal and vertical page reflections: Our method changes by less than 1 percentage point, classical XY-cut by 2 points, and LayoutReader-T by up to 8 points.

</details>


### [52] [Evidence-Supported Credit Risk Report Generation Using News-Centric Financial Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01023)
*Rocio Jimenez-Villen,Ziwei Xu,Ying Chen,Oscar Araque,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FinKG-News框架，通过从新闻中自动提取事件作为锚点并链接到公司，构建以公司为中心、感知环境的事实知识图谱。基于此图谱作为基础证据，开发了一种上下文学习架构用于信用风险报告生成，在三个核心金融维度上持续优于基线方法，质量提升19%-34%，同时减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场受新闻报道的真实事件驱动，但这些驱动因素在文本中通常隐含不明。现有方法未能显式建模事件与市场之间的关联，需要构建事实性、以公司为中心且感知环境的知识图谱来更好地解释市场动态。

Method: 提出FinKG-News框架，自动构建事件-公司链接的知识图谱；基于该图谱作为基础证据，设计上下文学习架构，用于信用风险报告生成，覆盖三个核心金融维度。

Result: 自动和人工评估表明，自动化幻觉检测和质量评估仍不可靠，专家判断不可或缺。所提方法持续优于基线，质量提升19%-34%，同时减少幻觉。

Conclusion: FinKG-News框架通过显式建模事件-市场关系，有效提升了信用风险报告生成的质量并降低了幻觉；但自动化评估工具尚不成熟，仍需专家参与。

Abstract: Financial markets evolve in response to real-world events reported in news, yet these drivers often remain implicit in text. To better explain market dynamics, event-market relations must be explicitly modeled through factual, company-centric, and environment-aware knowledge graphs. We present FinKG-News, a framework that automatically constructs such graphs by extracting news events as anchors linked to companies. Using FinKG-News as grounded evidence that integrates events, news, and company data, we develop an in-context learning architecture for credit risk report generation across three core financial dimensions. Automatic and human evaluations show that automated hallucination detection and quality assessment remain unreliable, making expert judgment indispensable. Our approach consistently outperforms baselines, improving quality by 19%-34% while reducing hallucinations. The source code and project resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/ichise-laboratory/FINKG-news.

</details>


### [53] [Behavior-Adaptive Conversational Agents: Toward a Fluid Personality Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01034)
*Hasibur Rahman,Smit Desai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种流体人格框架，让基于LLM的对话代理能够根据任务情境、用户目标和紧急程度，动态调整其隐喻角色（如教练、导师）和人格表达强度（低/中/高），以解决当前代理固定人格与情境不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM驱动的对话代理通常固定了人格和表达风格，无法适应不同任务情境（如医疗信息查询、健身指导、反思学习）中动态变化的紧迫性、正式程度和用户需求，导致人机交互失调。

Method: 提出Fluid Personality Framework，该框架联合调整两个维度：(1) 代理的隐喻角色（如教练、导师、图书管理员、工具），(2) 人格表达强度（低、中、高），调整依据为任务上下文、用户目标与特质、情境紧迫性。

Result: 现有证据表明适度人格表达优于极端高低表达（在信任、享受和采纳意愿上），且情境适切的隐喻优于静态单一角色（在用户体验和使用率上）；但当前大多数代理仍固定人格与风格。

Conclusion: 通过提出该框架及其核心设计维度，为未来构建更具情境适应性的对话代理提供了设计方向和理论基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based conversational agents (CAs) are now ubiquitous, creating new opportunities for AI-mediated behavior change. Their capacity to project nuanced personalities and adopt diverse metaphorical roles raises a design question: how should an agent's persona and personality be calibrated to the moment? Recent evidence suggests that (i) moderate personality expression outperforms low or high extremes on trust, enjoyment, and intention to adopt in goal-oriented tasks, and (ii) context-appropriate metaphors outperform static one-note assistants on user experience and uptake. Yet most CAs still fix both persona and style, risking misalignment when dynamics, urgency, and formality vary, for example in medical information seeking, fitness coaching, and reflective learning. We propose a Fluid Personality Framework that jointly adapts (1) the agent's metaphorical persona, such as coach, tutor, librarian, or tool, and (2) its personality expression intensity, low, medium, or high, as a function of task context, user goals and traits, and situational urgency. We sketch the framework and its core design dimensions.

</details>


### [54] [Conversable Complexity: Agentic LLM Collectives as Interpretable Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01047)
*Elias Najarro,Ane Espeseth,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将大语言模型（LLM）智能体集合作为人工生命（ALife）研究的计算基质，利用自然语言可解释性优势，通过文本痕迹和直接询问智能体来解读集体行为，并综述了相关现有研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂性与可解释性难以兼得：能够产生复杂行为的系统通常过于不透明而难以探究，而透明的系统又过于简单无法产生复杂现象。作者旨在利用LLM智能体集合在保持复杂行为的同时，通过自然语言通信实现直接可解释性。

Method: 将静态LLM转变为具有持久记忆、工具、共享技能和主动行动能力的智能体，形成智能体集合；利用自然语言作为通信媒介，通过文本痕迹和直接询问智能体来解读集体行为。

Result: 智能体集合中的LLM展现出孤立模型所不具备的涌现动力学；从受控实验到实际部署的现有研究已初步实现了这一智能体基质的概念。

Conclusion: LLM智能体集合可作为ALife研究的有效计算基质，其自然语言通信特性为集体行为的可解释性提供了独特优势。

Abstract: Complexity and interpretability rarely coincide: systems rich enough for complex behaviours to emerge are usually too opaque to question, while transparent ones are too simple for anything complex to emerge. A single large language model (LLM) is a static artefact, hardly exhibiting any of the emergent properties we associate with life. This changes through interaction: populations of LLMs display emergent dynamics absent from isolated models. Furthermore, LLMs can be endowed with persistent memory, tools and shared skills, and the capacity to initiate actions unprompted, i.e., turning LLMs agentic. In this paper, we argue that such collectives of agents can serve as a computational substrate for Artificial Life (ALife) research. Critically, since the agents communicate in natural language, their collective behaviour can be directly interrogated by examining textual traces and asking the agents themselves. We outline the notion of interpretability in language-model research and extend it for collectives of agents. Lastly, we survey recent examples of agentic LLM collectives that already instantiate the idea of agentic substrates, from controlled experiments to deployments in the wild.

</details>


### [55] [Message Passing Enables Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01077)
*Xuecheng Liu,Daman Arora,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出消息传递语言模型（MPLMs）框架，通过轻量级发送/接收原语实现LLM线程间直接通信，引入减少冗余上下文共享和抢占机制，在数独、3-SAT和长上下文问答三类任务上证明比串行CoT和并行FJ方法更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时缩放提升了大语言模型推理能力，但生成长思维链存在计算瓶颈。现有串行CoT方法效率低，而并行fork-join方法中线程间缺乏点对点通信，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出MPLM框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 通过避免冗余上下文共享降低通信成本；(2) 抢占机制允许线程基于部分信息提前终止。框架采用轻量级发送(send)和接收(recv)原语实现线程间直接通信。

Result: 在数独上，MPLM所需上下文渐近小于串行CoT和并行FJ；微调后能解决25x25数独（标准方法及无工具前沿模型难以处理）。在3-SAT上，抢占机制可终止无望分支提升效率。在长上下文QA上，通过适当提示，预训练模型遵循MPLM协议达到与FJ方法相当的效果。

Conclusion: MPLM通过线程间直接通信和抢占机制，在多个推理任务上实现了比现有串行和并行方法更高的效率与可扩展性，为LLM推理的并行缩放提供了新范式。

Abstract: While inference-time scaling has improved the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), the need to generate long chains-of-thought (CoTs) is a computational bottleneck. Thus, in contrast to sequential scaling methods like CoT, recent parallel scaling techniques instead use fork and join (FJ) primitives to divide work across multiple LLM threads. However, in the fork-join paradigm, threads are typically transient and do not communicate pointwise with one another which limits scalability. To tackle this, we introduce Message Passing Language Models (MPLMs), a framework for LLM reasoning in which threads communicate directly via lightweight send and receive primitives. MPLMs enable efficient scaling through two key mechanisms: (1) reduced communication costs, achieved by avoiding redundant context sharing, and (2) preemption, which allows threads to terminate early based on partial information from their peers.
  We demonstrate the promise of MPLMs on 3 classes of tasks. First, on Sudoku puzzles, we show that MPLMs require an asymptotically smaller context than both serial CoT and parallel FJ. We then fine-tune a single model to solve 25 x 25 puzzles that remain challenging for standard CoT and FJ approaches, as well as frontier reasoning models without tools. Second, on 3-SAT puzzles, the capability of preemption allows termination of unpromising branches, which results in improved efficiency. Finally, we show that appropriately prompted large pre-trained models follow the MPLM protocol, achieving competitive results on long-context question answering relative to popular fork-join approaches.

</details>


### [56] [Clinician-Level Agreement Without Clinical Caution: LLM Evaluator Limits in Medical AI Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01103)
*William Philipp,Finn Fassbender,Thorsten Langer,Martje Pauly,Rebecca Herzog,Alexander Baumann,Markus Hobert,Theresa Paulus,Ip Chi Wang,Lukas Goede,Johanna Reimer,Sebastian Löns,Ronald Böck,Sebastian Fudickar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MedQADE，首个德语开放式临床评估基准，包含 3,800 个由十名医生和九个 LLM 评估的样本。最佳评估模型 Gemini 3 Flash 的评分一致性接近医生间一致性，但自动评估器完全缺乏临床元认知（从不弃权），且存在系统性的模型家族偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式评估比选择题基准具有更强的临床有效性，但评分瓶颈推动了自动化 LLM-as-a-Judge 方法的应用，然而这类评估器是否能够复制临床校准和谨慎性尚未经过检验。德语作为主要临床语言缺乏原生评估基础设施。

Method: 构建 MedQADE 基准，包含 3,800 个开放式临床项目，由十名执业医师和九个 LLM 评估器进行标注。比较评估器与医生间的一致性（κ 系数），分析弃权行为与难度之间的关系，并量化模型家族依赖偏差。

Result: Gemini 3 Flash 达到 κ=0.694，接近医生间一致性 κ=0.709，但置信区间宽。自动评估器几乎完全缺乏临床元认知：医生随题目难度增加弃权率，而前沿模型在所有情况下都给出确定分数。存在系统性的模型家族依赖偏差，模型倾向于给架构相关的模型更高分数。

Conclusion: 统计上的一致性并不确保临床谨慎性，评估器的独立性需要显式验证。

Abstract: Open-response evaluation provides stronger clinical validity than multiple-choice benchmarks but creates a scoring bottleneck that motivates automated LLM-asa-Judge approaches. Whether such evaluators replicate clinical calibration and caution, however, remains untested. We introduce MedQADE, the first standardised open-response clinical benchmark for German, a major clinical language lacking native evaluation infrastructure, comprising 3,800 items annotated by ten practising physicians and nine Large Language Model (LLM) evaluators. The top-performing evaluator model, Gemini 3 Flash, reached alignment consistent with the physician ceiling (\k{appa} = 0.694 vs. \k{appa} = 0.709), though wide confidence intervals limit interpretation. Despite this statistical alignment, automated evaluators exhibited near-absent clinical metacognition: physicians scaled abstention with item difficulty, while frontier models assigned definitive scores in every case. We additionally quantified systematic lineage-dependent biases, where models preferentially scored architectural siblings, an effect independent of language. These results show that statistical alignment does not ensure clinical caution, and that evaluator independence requires explicit verification.

</details>


### [57] [Towards Developing a Multimodal Chat Assistant for University Stakeholders: RAG-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01115)
*Md Abu Hanif Shaikh,Abdullah Al Shafi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的多模态高校聊天机器人系统，结合大语言模型与语义检索，支持文本和图像查询，在降低幻觉率（从31.7%降至6.6%）的同时保持高满意度，适用于资源受限的开发中国家高校环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展中国家高校利益相关者难以获取及时可靠的信息，且现有基于规则的聊天机器人无法处理复杂的领域特定查询，也难以适应不断变化的院校政策。

Method: 系统将大语言模型与语义检索相结合，通过视觉-语言模型接受文本和图像查询，采用量化推理实现受限硬件上的快速部署，后端基于FastAPI构建，前端使用Next.js开发。

Result: 多模态评估显示系统在文本和图像查询上均保持高满意度；定量评估表明幻觉率从31.7%降至6.6%，验证了检索增强的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的RAG多模态高校聊天机器人有效解决了高校信息查询中的准确性和可靠性问题，显著降低了幻觉率，适合在发展中国家的资源受限环境中部署。

Abstract: University stakeholders often face difficulties in accessing timely and reliable information, especially in developing countries, where there are very few intelligent support systems. Existing rule-based chatbots are unable to handle complex, domain-specific queries and are not well-equipped to adapt to evolving institutional policies. As a fill-in-the-gap solution, we present the multimodal university chatbot with retrieval-augmented generation. The system combines the large language model with semantic retrieval to produce context-based responses from institution-centric resources, such as the university handbook. The system accepts text and image queries through the vision-language model and applies quantized inference for rapid deployment on constrained hardware. A scalable backend built with FastAPI, adjoined with a responsive frontend developed with Next.js, ensures real-time usability. Our multimodal evaluation demonstrates that the system maintains strong satisfaction scores across both text and image queries, despite increased response time for visual inputs. Furthermore, quantitative evaluation shows that hallucination is reduced from 31.7% to 6.6% in our proposed RAG-based system, confirming the effectiveness of retrieval grounding.

</details>


### [58] [$\text{Log}_\text{b}$Quant: Quantizing Language Models in Logarithmic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01127)
*Jeremias Bohn,Tizian Dippold,Mahdi Koubaa,Elias R. Wahl,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LogbQuant是一种基于可调底数对数量化的方法，在4位精度下优于非对称线性量化，实现内存节省与推理加速，适用于消费级GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有均匀量化方法由于低频率高幅度权重的存在，容易导致次优表示，需要更好的量化方案。

Method: 提出LogbQuant，一种可调底数的对数量化方法，以适应常见参数分布。

Result: 在4位精度下，LogbQuant在多个性能基准上优于非对称线性量化（张量级粒度），同时实现适度加速和高内存节省。

Conclusion: LogbQuant适合在消费级GPU上进行私有部署，兼顾性能、速度与内存效率。

Abstract: Quantization has become an invaluable tool to reduce memory requirements and inference speed of modern language models, in particular to make them available for consumer setups and edge devices. While previous work has primarily focused on uniform quantization codebooks, such approaches are prone to suboptimal representations due to low-frequency high-magnitude weights. We introduce Log$_\text{b}$Quant, a novel logarithmic quantization approach with adjustable bases, to adapt to common parameter distributions. We show that our method exhibits superior performance at 4-bit precision on several performance benchmarks compared to asymmetric linear quantization at tensor-wise granularity, while achieving moderate speedup and high memory savings, making it suitable for private use on consumer-grade GPUs.

</details>


### [59] [AGC-Bench: Measuring Artificial General Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01152)
*Roger Beaty,Vijeta Deshpande,Clin K. Y. Lai,Anna Attuch,Namrata Shivagunde,Swastik Roy,Rajkumar Pujari,Paul V. DiStefano,Sherin Muckatira,Claire E. Stevenson,Mikhail Gronas,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AGC-Bench，一个基于系统文献综述构建的人工通用创造力基准，涵盖78个数据集和多个创造力领域。通过因子分析发现LLM存在类似人类"g"因子的创造力"c"因子，并开发了AGC-Judge裁判模型。实验表明提示"要创造性"比启用推理更能提升表现，且人类顶尖水平仍领先LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创造力研究长期争论其是否领域特异性以及是否与一般智力可分离，这些争议现在同样适用于LLM，但缺乏统一的AI创造力基准来衡量和比较不同模型。

Method: 系统筛选3101篇论文、497个基准构建AGC-Bench（78个数据集，覆盖头脑风暴、问题解决、STEM、叙事、比喻语言、幽默等领域）；采用裁判响应理论校准LLM评判偏差；用校正后的评分微调Qwen3-30B得到AGC-Judge；对83个LLM进行因子分析。

Result: 因子分析发现单一创造力因子"c"解释81.5%方差，与一般知识/推理相关但可分离；提示"要创造性"比启用推理更有效提升表现；顶尖人类仍领先顶尖LLM。

Conclusion: LLM存在类似人类"g"因子的创造力"c"因子，创造力与一般能力可分离，提示工程比推理工程更能激发LLM创造力，当前LLM创造力尚未超越人类顶尖水平。

Abstract: Creativity research has debated whether creativity is domain-specific (e.g., visual, writing, science), and if it is psychometrically separable from general intelligence. Both questions now apply to LLMs, but a unified benchmark of AI creativity remains elusive. We introduce AGC-Bench, an artificial general creativity benchmark built from a systematic review of the AI creativity literature (3,101 papers screened, 497 benchmarks identified), paired with an agentic harness that converts idiosyncratic codebases into HELM-standardized benchmarks. The first release covers 78 datasets spanning brainstorming, problem solving, STEM, narrative, figurative language, and humor. To address bias in LLM-as-judge, we apply Judge Response Theory -- a psychometric calibration of judge leniency/severity; we then fine-tune Qwen3-30B on the bias-corrected ratings of three frontier LLMs to produce AGC-Judge, an open-weight model that robustly scores new creativity benchmarks it was not trained on. Results reveal frontier models at the top of the AGC-Bench leaderboard, with open models close behind. LLMs show different creative strengths, ranking higher on some domains (e.g., writing) than others (e.g., scientific ideation). Extensive experiments yield three main findings. First, applying factor analysis across 83 LLMs, we recover a single creativity factor 'c', analogous to the 'g' factor of general intelligence, that explains 81.5% of variance, related to but separable from general knowledge/reasoning. Second, we show that prompting models to "be creative" boosts their performance far more than enabling reasoning, evidence that the benchmark tracks creativity over general ability. Third, on a human-matched subset, we find the top human still leads the top LLM on creativity. We release AGC-Bench with a public leaderboard, AGC-Judge, and human data as open infrastructure for measuring AI creativity at scale.

</details>


### [60] [Adversarial Pragmatics for AI Safety Evaluation: A Benchmark for Instruction Conflict, Embedded Commands, and Policy Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01153)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗语用学作为评估语言模型在指令冲突、嵌入命令、引用、范围歧义、指示语、间接言语行为及多轮代理对话中行为的基准与标注协议，将语言判断方法论转化为验证安全评估、LLM评判器、黄金集构建、提示注入测试和安全文档的实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估将模糊的自然语言行为（如是否遵循指令、恰当拒绝、遵守政策等）压缩为二元通过/失败标签，忽略了失败源于能力限制、政策歧义、指令冲突、脚手架故障还是评估者判断不稳定。

Method: 构建了语言学控制的分类体系、18项种子基准（含验证器强制元数据）、54行本地种子试点、区分任务成功/政策合规/安全风险/拒绝结果/评估者信心的专家评估协议，以及评判者有效性、诊断歧义性和分类漂移的度量指标。

Result: 提供了一个实证和方法论贡献：将语言判断方法论转化为可实践的安全评估验证工具。

Conclusion: 该框架可系统性地用于验证安全评估、LLM评判器、黄金集构建、提示注入测试和安全文档的有效性。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for language models increasingly depend on judgments about ambiguous natural-language behaviour: whether a model has followed an instruction, refused appropriately, complied with a policy, resisted an embedded command, or misreported progress in an agentic task. Existing benchmarks often compress these distinctions into pass/fail labels, obscuring whether failures arise from capability limits, policy ambiguity, instruction conflict, scaffold failure, or unstable evaluator judgments.
  This paper introduces adversarial pragmatics as a benchmark and annotation protocol for evaluating model behaviour under instruction conflict, embedded commands, quotation, scope ambiguity, deixis, indirect speech acts, and multi-turn agent transcripts. The contribution is empirical and methodological: a linguistically controlled taxonomy, an 18-item seed benchmark with validator-enforced metadata, a 54-row local seed pilot, an expert-evaluation protocol distinguishing task success, policy compliance, safety risk, refusal outcome, and evaluator confidence, and metrics for judge validity, diagnostic ambiguity, and taxonomy drift. The framework turns linguistic judgment methodology into a practical tool for validating safety evals, LLM judges, gold-set construction, prompt-injection tests, and safety documentation.

</details>


### [61] [Distill to Detect: Exposing Stealth Biases in LLMs through Cartridge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01208)
*Shayan Talaei,Abhinav Chinta,Devvrit Khatri,Amin Karbasi,Azalia Mirhoseini,Amin Saberi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Distill to Detect (D2D)方法，通过将可疑模型与其基础模型之间的分布偏移蒸馏到KV缓存前缀适配器（cartridge）中，放大隐蔽的偏好偏见信号，使其在生成文本中可被检测。理论框架通过Fisher加权投影解释logit分布偏移，实证支持该方法能有效检测多种隐蔽偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在关键场景中可能偏好特定实体或观点，这种隐蔽偏见通过上下文蒸馏可迁移到语义无关数据上，仅存在于软logit分布中，无法通过文本检查发现。防御方存在根本性不对称：在不了解偏见主题的情况下，任何检测方法都无法可靠地发现隐蔽的偏好偏见。

Method: Distill to Detect (D2D)方法：将可疑模型与基础模型之间的分布偏移蒸馏到cartridge（KV缓存前缀适配器）中，集中主导散度并放大偏见信号到生成文本中。理论框架基于Fisher加权投影对logit分布偏移的视角。

Result: D2D成功放大了隐蔽模型的隐藏偏见，使其能够在多种偏见类型下被可靠检测。Fisher加权投影的理论框架得到实证观察支持。

Conclusion: 通过将前缀调优适配器的容量瓶颈转化为检测工具，D2D为审计部署语言模型中的隐蔽行为提供了实用构建模块，解决了防御方在不了解偏见主题时检测隐蔽偏好偏见的根本性难题。

Abstract: Language models deployed in high-stakes roles can potentially favor certain entities, brands, or viewpoints, steering user decisions at scale. Such preferential biases can be introduced by any actor in the model's supply chain and are most dangerous when the model reveals its preference only on the relevant topic while behaving identically to its unmodified base on all other inputs. Recent work has shown that these biases can transfer through context distillation on semantically unrelated data, with the signal residing entirely in the soft logit distribution and remaining invisible to text-based inspection. However, the defender faces a fundamental asymmetry: without knowing the bias topic, no detection method can reliably surface a stealth preferential bias, regardless of whether it examines generated text, internal representations, or model weights. Here we introduce Distill to Detect (D2D), a method that surfaces hidden biases by distilling the distributional shift between a suspected model and its base into a cartridge (a KV-cache prefix adapter), concentrating the dominant divergence and amplifying the bias signal into generated text. We show that D2D successfully amplifies the hidden biases of stealth models to the extent that they can be reliably detected across multiple bias types. We also propose a theoretical framework that explains the efficacy of D2D through the lens of Fisher-weighted projection of the logit distribution shift, supported by empirical observations. By turning the capacity bottleneck of prefix-tuning adapters into a detection tool, D2D provides a practical building block for auditing hidden behaviors in deployed language models.

</details>


### [62] [The State-Prediction Separation Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01218)
*Giovanni Monea,Nathan Godey,Kianté Brantley,Yoav Artzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出状态-预测分离假设，将Transformer中预测下一个token和存储有用状态的双重角色解耦，通过双计算流设计在预训练实验中持续提升数据与计算效率，下游任务平均提升2-3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer使用同一前向计算流同时承担预测下一token和存储对未来预测有用的状态两种功能，作者认为这种耦合可能限制了语言建模性能，因此提出假设将二者分离可以带来更好的效果。

Method: 设计了一种双计算流的Transformer变体，将预测token和存储状态两个功能分别由不同的计算流承担，并在多种规模下进行预训练实验验证。

Result: 状态-预测分离在数据效率和计算效率上持续提升，验证损失改善，在下游任务上平均超越标准Transformer 2-3个百分点。

Conclusion: 状态-预测分离假设成立，解耦token预测与状态存储两个角色能够带来更好的语言建模性能，且通过消融实验排除了潜在混淆因素，证明了该设计在梯度上的根本性差异。

Abstract: Transformers use the same forward computation stream to both predict the next token and store useful state for future token predictions. We formulate the \emph{state-prediction separation hypothesis}: disentangling the two roles yields better language modeling performance. We design a Transformer variant that uses two computation streams to separate the two functions, and conduct pretraining experiments across various scales. Our experiments show that state-prediction separation consistently offers better data and compute efficiencies, improving validation loss and outperforming standard Transformers by 2--3 percentage points on average on downstream tasks. We also conduct extensive empirical analysis that rules out potential confounders and demonstrates the fundamental difference in the gradients our design entails.

</details>


### [63] [Measuring the Gap Between Human and LLM Research Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01233)
*Ziyu Chen,Yilun Zhao,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个大规模评估框架，通过对比LLM生成的研究想法与真实人类论文的分布差异，发现LLM想法过度集中于"桥接型机会"和"综合方法"，而人类想法分布更广泛，表明LLM的研究品味存在系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多从新颖性、可行性或专家偏好判断单个想法，但缺乏对LLM生成想法与人类研究者之间整体差距的量化表征。

Method: 从高质量人类论文逆向工程出可能启发核心想法的少量相关工作，让LLM从论文标题和摘要中生成新想法；引入双轴研究品味分类法（机会模式与研究范式），量化人类与LLM想法的分布差异。

Result: LLM想法不成比例地集中在桥接型机会和综合方法上，而人类论文参考分布更广泛地分布于多种问题框架和贡献构建方式。

Conclusion: 强大的LLM能产生一系列合理想法，但其分布范围比人类研究品味更窄且存在系统性偏移。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to brainstorm research ideas, but existing evaluations mostly judge individual ideas by novelty, feasibility, or expert preference. We instead ask: how far are current LLM-generated ideas from human researchers? To characterize this gap, we build a large-scale evaluation framework for ideation from high-quality human research papers. For each paper, we reverse-engineer a small set of closely related prior works that likely inspired its core idea. LLMs are then prompted to generate a new idea from the set of paper titles and summaries. We introduce a two-axis research-taste taxonomy to profile each idea by its opportunity pattern and research paradigm, and use it to quantify the divergence between human and LLM ideas. Across idea sets generated by different LLMs, we observe a consistent distributional gap: LLM ideas are disproportionately concentrated around bridge-like opportunities and synthesis methods, whereas the human paper reference distribution spreads more broadly across ways of framing gaps and constructing contributions. This result suggests that strong LLMs can produce a range of reasonable ideas, but that range remains narrower than, and systematically shifted relative to, human research taste.

</details>

### [64] [SmoothAgent: Efficient Long-Horizon LLM-Based Agent Serving with Lookahead Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00151)
*Zaifeng Pan,Qianxu Wang,Zhengding Hu,Chang Chen,Yue Guan,Yanbo Zhou,Steven Swanson,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种前瞻性编程模型，通过将上下文转换作为异步操作预执行并缓存KV，有效消除LLM Agent系统中上下文工程带来的TTFT开销，加速高达11.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Agent框架依赖上下文工程策略（卸载、缩减、隔离）控制上下文长度，但这些策略的上下文转换会导致KV缓存失效和重新预填充，显著增加TTFT，影响模型质量和用户体验。

Method: 识别上下文转换具有段可分解性（前缀转换独立于未来token），基于此提出前瞻性编程模型：将上下文转换表达为异步操作，运行时提前执行转换并预生成KV缓存，实现直接替换而不阻塞；设计前瞻感知调度器，在LLM服务系统中支持异步请求与延迟关键型负载共存并控制干扰。

Result: 实现了对代表性上下文工程策略的支持，集成到现有Agent框架和LLM服务系统中，消除转换开销，TTFT降低高达11.9倍。

Conclusion: 提出的前瞻性方法有效解决了Agent系统中上下文工程导致的性能瓶颈，通过异步预转换和KV缓存重用大幅降低首token延迟。

Abstract: LLM-based agents execute multi-turn workflows with continuously growing contexts, where LLM calls are interleaved with tool invocations and environment feedback. To maintain model quality, modern agent frameworks rely on context engineering strategies such as offloading, reduction, and isolation to control the context length. However, these strategies introduce significant context transformation overhead: each transformation invalidates existing KV caches and triggers re-prefill, leading to increased time-to-first-token (TTFT).
  In this paper, we identify that context transformations are segment-decomposable, where the transformation of a prefix is independent of future tokens. This property enables transformations to be executed ahead of time. Based on this insight, we propose a lookahead programming model that allows agent frameworks to express context transformations as asynchronous operations without modifying their execution logic. The runtime proactively executes these transformations and prepares transformed KV caches in advance, enabling direct context replacement without blocking. We further design a lookahead-aware scheduler in LLM serving systems to support these asynchronous requests alongside latency-critical workloads with controlled interference. We implement our approach to support representative context engineering strategies and integrate it into existing agent frameworks and LLM serving systems. Experiments show that our approach effectively eliminates transformation overhead and reduces TTFT by up to 11.9x.

</details>


### [65] [Promise-Future Synchronization for Cluster Asynchronous Many-Task Runtimes via MPI One-Sided Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00303)
*Mia Reitz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 在ItoyoriFBC AMT运行时中，将原有的仅future模型扩展为promise-future模型，支持动态依赖创建，并在分层LU分解（HLU）算法上实现了近理想的15.6倍加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的ItoyoriFBC AMT运行时使用仅future模型，每个future在创建时绑定其生产者，且读取每个future的任务数量必须在编译时固定。这导致无法直接表达动态创建依赖关系的算法，限制了其适用性。

Method: 在ItoyoriFBC运行时中实现promise-future模型，解除future与生产者的绑定，并允许运行时动态确定读取future的任务数量，从而支持动态依赖关系。

Result: 使用分层LU分解（HLU）算法在最多16节点上进行实验评估，观察到近理想的扩展性能，获得15.6倍的加速比。

Conclusion: 通过引入promise-future模型，ItoyoriFBC运行时能够支持需要动态创建依赖的算法（如HLU），并实现了良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes use futures as placeholders for values produced by other tasks. In the ItoyoriFBC AMT runtime, the existing future-only model binds each future to its producer at creation time and requires the number of tasks that read each future to be fixed at compile time. This prevents directly expressing algorithms that create dependencies dynamically. We extend ItoyoriFBC with an implementation of a promise-future model that lifts these limitations. Thereby, our ItoyoriFBC variant supports dynamic algorithms such as Hierarchical LU factorization (HLU). We experimentally evaluated our implementation using HLU on up to 16 nodes and observed near-ideal scaling with a 15.6x speedup.

</details>


### [66] [CloudyGUI: A Novel Python-based Framework for Auto-Scaling and Cloud Workload Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00455)
*Jyoti Bawa,Mohit Kaushik,Kuljit Kaur Chahal,Kamaljit Kaur*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CloudyGUI是一个Python模拟框架，提供易于使用的GUI，通过三阶段流水线（负载生成、预测、自动缩放）帮助研究人员测试云资源管理策略，经内部、中间和外部验证，其生成负载与真实数据高度吻合，专家评审证实其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云环境的高度动态性给资源管理带来巨大挑战，准确的工作负载预测对实现有效的自动缩放至关重要，但缺乏一个易用且高效的模拟平台来测试和验证相关策略。

Method: 采用三阶段流水线：负载生成、基于XGBoost和LSTM的预测、基于MAPE循环的自动缩放系统；并通过内部、中间和外部三种验证方法确保系统可靠性。

Result: 生成负载与真实数据集高度匹配，双样本K-S检验显示CPU p值为0.19、内存p值为0.14；GUI相较于命令行工具仅增加1.4x-4.67x的额外开销；专家评审验证了工具的真实性和实用性。

Conclusion: CloudyGUI填补了云应用自动缩放模拟领域的关键空白，为研究人员提供了一个易用高效的平台来开发先进的云管理解决方案。

Abstract: Purpose: Cloud computing environments are highly dynamic, creating major challenges for resource management. Accurate workload prediction is therefore essential for effective auto-scaling. To address this, we present CloudyGUI, a Python simulation framework with an easy-to-use GUI that allows researchers to test and validate resource management strategies. Methods: This framework employs a three-stage pipeline: workload generation, prediction (utilizing XGBoost and LSTM), and an auto-scaling system based on the MAPE loop. Validation includes internal, intermediate, and external methods to ensure system reliability. Results: CloudyGUI's generated workloads closely match real-world datasets. A two-sample K-S test confirms this alignment, showing strong p-values of 0.19 for CPU and 0.14 for memory. When compared to a command-line tool, the GUI adds only a minimal overhead of 1.4x-4.67x. Furthermore, expert review validates the tool's realism and practical usefulness. Conclusion: CloudyGUI fills a critical gap by providing an accessible and efficient platform for simulating auto-scaling in cloud applications, helping researchers develop advanced cloud management solutions.

</details>


### [67] [ELDR: Expert-Locality-Aware Decode Routing for PD-Disaggregated MoE Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00466)
*Sangjin Choi,Sukmin Cho,Yifan Xiong,Ziyue Yang,Youngjin Kwon,Peng Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ELDR是一种面向预填充-解码分离的MoE模型服务系统的专家局部性感知解码路由器，通过利用请求的专家签名进行离线聚类和在线局部性感知路由，在保持模型输出不变的前提下，在40GPU规模部署中相比最优负载均衡基线降低中位Token处理时间5.9-13.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PD分离的MoE模型服务中，解码路由器仅考虑负载均衡，未考虑专家局部性：即使负载均衡，各解码worker因激活不同专家集合而需加载不同权重，导致推理延迟存在显著差异。

Method: 从请求的预填充阶段专家激活构建专家签名，预测生成阶段将激活的专家；通过平衡K-means聚类将签名空间划分给各解码worker；在线路由使用局部性感知带路由将请求发送到与其签名最匹配且负载最轻的worker；设计签名缓存与KV缓存共索引，在KV块粒度保持签名的精确性以支持前缀缓存。

Result: 在vLLM中实现，在最多40GPU部署上，跨三种MoE模型和两种工作负载，相比四种负载均衡基线中最强者，中位Token处理时间降低5.9-13.9%，且模型输出保持不变。

Conclusion: ELDR通过显式建模MoE模型的专家局部性，证明了在PD分离服务架构中，超越纯负载均衡的路由策略能够带来显著的端到端性能提升。

Abstract: In prefill-decode (PD) disaggregated LLM serving, each request is assigned to a decode worker after prefill. Existing decode routers balance only load; for mixture-of-experts (MoE) models this is incomplete: equally loaded workers can differ in latency, since each decode step loads the weights of every distinct expert its batch activates. We present ELDR, an expert-locality-aware decode router for PD-disaggregated MoE serving. From a request's prefill expert activations, ELDR builds an expert signature predicting the experts it will activate during generation. Offline, balanced K-means partitions signature space across decode workers; online, locality-band routing sends each request to the least-loaded worker among those best matching its signature. A signature cache, co-indexed with the KV cache at KV-block granularity, keeps signatures exact under prefix caching. Implemented in vLLM and evaluated on deployments of up to 40 GPUs, ELDR reduces median TPOT by 5.9-13.9% over the strongest of four load-balancing baselines across three MoE models and two workloads, with model outputs unchanged.

</details>


### [68] [Modeling and Chasing the Energy-Efficiency Sweet Spots in Modern GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00819)
*Ayesha Afzal,Markus Manfred Li,Michael Panzlaff*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究通过分子动力学基准测试和压力测试，在多种GPU（A40、A100、H100、H200）和CPU系统上分析了硬件配置对能耗的影响，发现能效存在由非线性GPU功率-频率缩放驱动的工作负载和架构依赖的转变区间，并提出了一个可解释的分析模型来识别最优能效运行点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构CPU-GPU高性能计算中能耗是关键限制因素，需要理解硬件配置如何在实际工作负载下影响能耗解，以指导能效优化。

Method: 使用GROMACS、AMBER分子动力学基准和FIRESTARTER压力测试，在配备A40、A100、H100、H200 GPU和Intel Ice Lake CPU的系统上，通过频率缩放和功率封顶进行实验。提出可解释分析模型，将GPU功率分解为线性和非线性分量。

Result: 能效存在工作负载和架构依赖的转变区间；模型拟合经验数据误差低；基线功率、非线性功率行为和转变频率是支配能效的关键参数；功率封顶效果通常不如频率降低有效。

Conclusion: 能效HPC执行是配置依赖问题，存在可识别的区间转变，模型可指导选择最优运行点。

Abstract: Energy consumption is a key limitation in high-performance computing on heterogeneous CPU-GPU systems. This work studies how hardware configuration affects energy-to-solution under realistic workloads. We study energy efficiency regimes using molecular dynamics benchmarks (GROMACS and AMBER) and a stress-test benchmark (FIRESTARTER) on systems with A40, A100, H100, and H200 GPUs and Intel Ice Lake CPU, varying frequency scaling and power cap. We show that energy-to-solution exhibits workload- and architecture-dependent transitions between efficient and inefficient regimes, driven by nonlinear GPU power-frequency scaling. We introduce an interpretable analytical model that decomposes GPU power into linear and nonlinear components, identifying a workload- and architecture-dependent transition frequency beyond which efficiency degrades. The model fits empirical data with low error and highlights the role of baseline power, nonlinear power behavior, and transition frequency as the dominant parameters governing energy efficiency. Power capping is generally less effective for efficiency tuning than frequency reduction, especially for workloads that operate far from thermal design power. Overall, energy-efficient HPC execution is a configuration-dependent problem with identifiable regime shifts, and we provide model-driven guidance for selecting operating points.

</details>


### [69] [Next-Generation Agentic Reinforcement Learning Systems Enable Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01120)
*Ran Yan,Wei Fu,Jiale Li,Shusheng Xu,Zhiyu Mei,Jiaxuan Gao,Jiarui Zhang,Xujie Shen,Hao Dai,Chuyi He,Zhen Pu,Jun Mei,Zhiyao Lin,Haitao Wang,Zhiqiang Ding,Jiawei Zhang,Huaijie Wang,Ruida Xu,Youhe Jiang,Yi Wu,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出，企业级大规模智能体服务的核心瓶颈不在强化学习算法本身，而在于缺乏支持在线RL的系统基础设施，具体表现为三大缺失：标准化的轨迹数据协议、企业级数据代理、统一的演化控制平面。文章提出新一代智能体RL系统需围绕这三根支柱协同设计，并通过AReaL2.0实例化其中一条技术路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM智能体在生产部署中仍然是静态的（模型权重、系统提示、工具集等在部署后冻结），任何改进都需要人工介入的离线循环。尽管已有自进化智能体的初步工作（如OpenClaw），但在企业级大规模部署场景下，缺乏能让智能体从自身部署经验中持续学习的在线RL系统。

Method: 论文采用论证加架构设计的方法：首先识别出当前智能体RL系统在三个关键方面的不足（轨迹数据协议、数据代理、演化控制平面），然后提出下一代系统需围绕这三个支柱进行协同设计，并给出具体架构、案例研究和反论证。最后通过AReaL2.0实例化，将现有RL基础设施重构为面向智能体的在线RL循环。

Result: 识别出企业级自进化智能体部署的三个系统级瓶颈，提出三支柱架构蓝图，并通过AReaL2.0展示了将RL基础设施重组为面向智能体的在线学习循环的可行路径。

Conclusion: 智能体能力的下一次飞跃将来自从自身经验中持续学习的系统，而实现这一愿景的关键障碍在于RL系统基础设施而非算法，下一代系统需要围绕标准化轨迹数据协议、企业级数据代理和统一演化控制平面进行协同设计。

Abstract: LLM agents are rapidly being deployed in production, including coding assistants, customer-support chatbots, and scientific research assistants, yet they remain fundamentally static in enterprise deployment. The LLM weights, system prompts, tool repertoires, and in-context harnesses are frozen at deployment time, and any improvement requires a manual loop of human-curated data collection, offline fine-tuning, modification of the agentic paradigm, and re-deployment. Recent work on self-evolving agents, such as OpenClaw for individual users, indicates that the next leap in agent capability will come from agents that continually learn from their own experience. In this paper, we argue that this vision for self-evolving agent deployment is being held back for enterprise-level large-scale agentic service not by reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms but by agentic online RL systems. Specifically, current agentic RL systems and the surrounding observability software stack are inadequate along three essential aspects: (i) there is no standardized agent trajectory data protocol capable of carrying RL learning signals at step granularity across heterogeneous agent paradigms; (ii) there is no enterprise-grade comprehensive data proxy that converts real workloads into governed learning substrates; and (iii) there is no unified agent evolution control plane that automatically decides, based on trajectory statistics, when to update policy weights or evolve the in-context harness. The next generation of agentic RL systems must be co-designed around these three pillars, and we sketch concrete architectures, case studies, and counter-arguments. We instantiate one branch through AReaL2.0, reorganizing existing RL infrastructure into an agent-oriented online RL loop for policy weight updates from deployed workloads.

</details>

### [70] [Representation as a Bottleneck for Mechanistic Interpretability: The Manifestation Unit Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00089)
*Hussein Chouman,Wataru Sasaki,Tomokazu Matsui,Hirohiko Suwa,Keiichi Yasumoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Manifestation Units协议——一种包含(E, S, R, D, G, T)字段的类型化元组协议，用于结构化组织和检索神经网络组件级可解释性分析结果，在视觉和语言模型上验证了其优于非结构化检索，并发现CNN过滤器满足因果充分性和必要性，以及(S+R)双字段核心不可约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性分析产出（选择性表、电路图、特征列表）无法跨研究复用，不可组合、不可通过自然语言查询，也无法直接用于下游审计或干预。需要一种独立的表示层来桥接分析与下游应用。

Method: 设计Manifestation Units类型化元组协议（E: entity, S: scope, R: response, D: distribution, G: gain, T: attention-head primitives），通过混合检索自动填充结构化字段，在beta-VAE、CNN、GPT-2上实例化验证。

Result: 类型化结构在检索任务上显著优于非结构化基线；按模式检索的CNN过滤器满足因果充分性和必要性；协议可无缝吸收注意力头原语，在预算匹配控制下回溯恢复已知IOI电路成员；发现(S+R)双字段核心不可约，其余字段冗余或相互干扰。

Conclusion: 本文提供的是可解释性分析的架构基础设施而非前沿规模验证，所提出的协议可作为组件级分析的标准化输出格式，支持跨场景的复用、查询和下游干预。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has produced a rich inventory of component-level analyses that characterise what neural-network components encode and how they interact. Their outputs, however, are not easily reusable: selectivity tables, circuit diagrams, and feature lists remain locked in per-study notebooks - non-composable, not queryable in natural language, and not directly actionable for downstream audit or intervention. We study the representation layer that sits between these analyses and downstream use as a bottleneck that can be evaluated independently, and introduce Manifestation Units, a typed tuple protocol (E, S, R, D, G) extended with attention-head primitives (T) for transformer architectures, organising per-component statistics into structured fields populated automatically and queried through hybrid retrieval. Instantiated across generative vision (beta-VAE), discriminative vision (CNN), and language (GPT-2), the protocol supports two findings: typed structure substantially outperforms unstructured baselines on retrieval, and CNN filters retrieved by the schema satisfy causal sufficiency and necessity criteria under matched-budget controls. The schema absorbs attention-head primitives without modification, set-recovers known IOI circuit members under retrieval-budget-matched controls, and reveals an irreducible two-field core (S+R) with remaining fields either redundant or actively interfering. We present this as schema infrastructure for mechanistic interpretability rather than frontier-scale validation.

</details>


### [71] [SNAP-FM: Sparse Nonlinear Accelerated Projection for Physics-Constrained Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00095)
*Alaina Kolli,Theodoros Xenakis,Utkarsh Utkarsh,Pengfei Cai,Rafael Gomez-Bombarelli,Alan Edelman,Christopher Vincent Rackauckas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用稀疏GPU非线性优化（ExaModels.jl + MadNLP.jl）来加速物理约束生成模型（如PCFM）中的约束投影步骤，在保持约束满足的同时显著提升非线性约束处理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型作为物理仿真的可扩展替代方案，无法保证输出满足守恒律、边界条件和非线性不变量。现有约束采样方法在推理时精确强制执行这些约束，但投影、校正和轨迹优化步骤的计算成本高昂，尤其对于非线性约束，而标准ML框架的密集张量运算和稀疏求解器可组合性差，使得高效批处理非线性优化难以实现。

Method: 利用样本批处理和局部PDE耦合在投影子问题中自然诱导出的结构（块稀疏Jacobian和KKT系统），通过ExaModels.jl暴露该结构，并用MadNLP.jl和GPU稀疏分解求解产生的稀疏非线性规划问题。将其应用于Physics-Constrained Flow Matching (PCFM)。

Result: 在线性、非线性、一维和二维约束的PDE基准测试上，该方法在保持约束满足的同时加速了非线性约束投影。

Conclusion: 稀疏GPU非线性优化是科学机器学习中受约束生成采样的实用基础，为解决物理约束生成模型的计算瓶颈提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Generative models have emerged as scalable surrogates for physical simulation, yet they offer no guarantee that their outputs respect the conservation laws, boundary conditions, and nonlinear invariants that govern the underlying physics. Constrained sampling closes this gap, enforcing such constraints exactly at inference time without retraining, but at a computational cost: projection, correction, and trajectory-optimization steps are repeated during sampling, with these steps becoming expensive for nonlinear constraints. Standard ML frameworks exacerbate this: their dense tensor algebra and limited sparse solver composability obscure the structure that physical constraints naturally induce, making efficient batched nonlinear optimization difficult to realize in practice. We address this bottleneck by exploiting the structure that sample-wise batching and local PDE couplings induce in the projection subproblems -- namely, block-sparse Jacobian and KKT systems -- exposing this structure using ExaModels.jl and solving the resulting sparse nonlinear programs with MadNLP.jl and GPU sparse factorization. Applied to Physics-Constrained Flow Matching (PCFM), on PDE benchmarks with linear, nonlinear, one-dimensional, and two-dimensional constraints, this approach accelerates nonlinear constraint projection while maintaining constraint satisfaction. These results show that sparse GPU nonlinear optimization is a practical foundation for constrained generative sampling in scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [72] [SemiScope: Disentangling Classifier Tuning and Joint Optimization in Semi-Supervised Security Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00113)
*Rui Shu,Tianpei Xia,Jingzhu He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SemiScope分析框架，通过贝叶斯优化联合调整半监督学习管道各组件，发现大部分性能增益实际上来源于对下游分类器的超参数优化（HPO），而非SSL与分类器的复杂交互。简单的Self-Training + 调优分类器 + 调整决策阈值即可达到接近完整管道的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全领域的半监督学习（SSL）标签稀缺，但现有工作常将SSL作为黑盒使用（默认参数、固定分类器、忽略伪标签导致的类不平衡）。近期工作虽报告通过联合搜索/AutoML获得显著提升，但难以归因这些提升究竟来自SSL与分类器的有用交互，还是仅仅来自对下游分类器的调优。作者旨在解耦这些效应。

Method: 构建SemiScope分析工具，使用贝叶斯优化联合调优SSL设置、置信过滤、过采样和分类器。关键控制实验Tuned-Clf固定SSL为默认，但给分类器相同的100次贝叶斯优化预算和验证集阈值调优。在10%标签率下，使用配对TOST检验（±1.0 g-measure最小效应量）进行比较。实验在5个二元表格安全数据集上进行，使用经典SSL和基于树的分类器。

Result: SemiScope在所有5个数据集上击败所有默认SSL基线（提升0.7-12.7点）。但在等预算控制下，Tuned-Clf在4/5数据集上与完整管道统计等价（Phishing数据集无结论）。分类器HPO单独恢复了SemiScope相对于默认Self-Training + Random Forest增益的中位数86%。

Conclusion: 可重用贡献是分解协议。更简单的方案足够：使用Self-Training，用贝叶斯优化调优分类器，在验证集上调优决策阈值。该方案在4个数据集上20-30%标签率、Drebin上40%标签率即可达到监督RF在1 g-measure以内的性能，且在所有数据集上所需标签率低于或等于默认ST+RF。

Abstract: Background. Labeled data for security classification is scarce. Semi-supervised learning (SSL) propagates labels from a small labeled pool to larger unlabeled pools. Yet security applications often use SSL as a black box: default parameters, a fixed classifier, and no handling of pseudo-label-induced class imbalance. Aims. Recent work reports sizeable gains from optimizing SSL pipelines via joint search, AutoML, or per-component tuning. These gains are hard to attribute: they may reflect useful SSL-classifier interactions, or mostly from simply tuning the downstream classifier. We disentangle these effects for binary tabular security data with classical SSL and tree-based classifiers. Method. We build SemiScope as an analysis instrument, not a deployment recommendation. It uses Bayesian Optimization to jointly tune SSL settings, confidence filtering, oversampling, and the classifier. The key control, Tuned-Clf, fixes SSL to defaults but gets the same 100-trial classifier budget and validation-set threshold tuning as SemiScope. At 10% labels, we compare them with paired TOST using a +/-1.0 g-measure smallest effect of interest. Results. SemiScope beats every default SSL baseline on all five datasets, improving over the strongest by 0.7-12.7 points. Under the equal-budget control, Tuned-Clf is statistically equivalent to the full pipeline on 4 of 5 datasets; Phishing is inconclusive. Classifier HPO alone recovers a median 86% of SemiScope's gain over Default Self-Training (ST) + Random Forest (RF). Conclusions. The reusable contribution is the decomposition protocol. A simpler recipe suffices: use Self-Training, tune the classifier with Bayesian Optimization, and tune the decision threshold on validation data. It reaches within 1 g-measure of Supervised RF at 20-30% labels on four datasets and 40% on Drebin, at the same or lower label rate than Default ST + RF on every dataset.

</details>


### [73] [A Filtered Mixture-of-Generators for Fully Synthetic Survival Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00127)
*Niccolò Maria Rizzi,Eugenio Lomurno,Alberto Archetti,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoGS通过从多个生成器中采样并基于集成生存模型打分筛选，在小型生存分析数据集上实现了优于单一生成器的合成数据质量，显著提升了下游生存模型性能且未降低隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析面临训练数据昂贵且稀缺的问题——事件需多年随访积累、队列规模小、隐私法规限制跨机构共享。表格生成模型本身也依赖大量数据，在小样本场景下单一生成器难以准确刻画人群分布，导致下游模型性能下降。

Method: FoGS将合成数据构建重新定义为样本选择而非生成：从四种不同架构的表格生成器中抽取候选样本池，使用七种生存模型集成对每个样本进行评分（以适当评分规则作为样本合理性代理），通过双层优化——外层优化选择策略（生成器配额、评分器权重、随机补充、事件时间和删失的分层平衡），内层调优下游模型（XGBoost-Cox）。

Result: 在16个公开数据集上（训合成测真实，C-index和IBS指标0-100量表），FoGS平均C-index提升+2.17，IBS提升+0.67；9/16数据集两项指标均改进，13/16至少一项改进（单侧Wilcoxon p=0.039和p=0.035）；在大多数队列上匹配或超越真实数据训练效果，且与未过滤采样相比最近邻隐私边际无显著变化。

Conclusion: 在隐私受限的临床场景中，对异质生成器池进行样本过滤是真实数据训练的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Survival analysis models time-to-event data, but in clinical settings training data are costly and scarce: events accrue over years of follow-up, cohorts are small, and privacy regulations restrict sharing across institutions. Tabular generative models promise augmentation and privacy-preserving cohort sharing, yet are themselves data-hungry -- on the small cohorts typical of survival analysis, a single generator rarely characterizes the population well enough for downstream models trained on its output to match real-data performance. FoGS (Filtered Mixture-of-Generators for Survival analysis) reframes synthetic-data construction as sample selection rather than generation. A candidate pool is drawn from four architecturally distinct tabular generators, and each sample is scored by an ensemble of seven survival models trained on real data, using proper scoring rules as a per-sample plausibility proxy. A two-level pipeline optimizes, in its outer loop, a selection policy -- generator quotas, scorer weights, a random complement, and stratified balancing on event time and censoring -- against held-out downstream performance, while an inner loop tunes the downstream model (XGBoost-Cox). On 16 public datasets under train-on-synthetic, test-on-real (C-index and IBS, $0$--$100$ scale), FoGS yields mean improvements of $+2.17$ in C-index and $+0.67$ in IBS, improving both metrics on 9 of 16 datasets and at least one on 13 (one-sided Wilcoxon $p=0.039$ and $p=0.035$). It matches or exceeds real-data training on most cohorts, with no significant change in nearest-neighbour privacy margin relative to unfiltered sampling. Sample filtering over a heterogeneous generator pool is thus a viable substitute for real-data training in privacy-restricted clinical settings.

</details>


### [74] [GRPO, Dr. GRPO, and DAPO Are Three Operations on One Number: The Group-Standard-Deviation Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00152)
*Yong Yi Bay,Kathleen A. Yearick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 三种流行的语言模型推理训练方法（GRPO、Dr. GRPO、DAPO）看似不同，实则都是对同一核心量——标准偏差（即模型多次回答之间对错的离散程度）——进行不同设置。论文证明这些方法本质上是同一个调节旋钮的三个档位，且该旋钮直接决定了训练更新的幅度和位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示GRPO、Dr. GRPO、DAPO三种方法背后的统一数学原理，澄清它们并非各自独立的"技巧"，而是对同一关键量的不同处理方式。

Method: 通过理论推导证明三种方法的本质等价性：GRPO除以标准偏差，Dr. GRPO去掉除法，DAPO丢弃标准偏差为零的组。在Big-Math数据集上进行实证验证，并结合实际训练运行确认理论结果。
